\"server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/metadata/LifecycleExecutionState.java\",\n      \"symbol\": \"LifecycleExecutionState\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"text\": \"# Elasticsearch Index Lifecycle Management (ILM) Architecture Analysis\\n\\n## Overview\\nThe Elasticsearch 8.17.0 ILM system is a comprehensive policy-driven index management framework. The implementation spans across three main components: core policy definitions, ILM coordination service, and cluster state management.\\n\\n## Core Components\\n\\n### 1. Policy Model Classes (x-pack/plugin/core/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/xpack/core/ilm/)\\n\\n**LifecyclePolicy** (LifecyclePolicy.java)\\n- Represents the lifecycle of an index from creation to deletion\\n- Contains a mapping of phase names to Phase objects\\n- Implements SimpleDiffable<LifecyclePolicy> and ToXContentObject for serialization\\n- Supports TimeseriesLifecycleType and validates phases through LifecycleType\\n- Includes optional metadata and deprecation flags\\n\\n**Phase** (Phase.java)\\n- Represents a set of LifecycleActions to execute at a particular point in index lifecycle\\n- Contains minimum age (TimeValue) and a map of named LifecycleActions\\n- Implements ToXContentObject and Writeable for serialization\\n- Ordered by LifecycleType; actions execute in defined order\\n\\n**Step** (Step.java)\\n- Abstract base class for all ILM execution steps\\n- Contains StepKey (phase, action, name) and next step reference\\n- Subclassed into four main step types:\\n  - ClusterStateActionStep: Updates cluster state, immediate execution\\n  - ClusterStateWaitStep: Waits for cluster state condition\\n  - AsyncActionStep: Performs asynchronous actions via client\\n  - AsyncWaitStep: Periodically checks asynchronous conditions\\n\\n### 2. Step Execution Framework\\n\\n**ClusterStateActionStep** (ClusterStateActionStep.java)\\n- Abstract class for steps that modify cluster state directly\\n- Method: performAction(Index, ClusterState) -> ClusterState\\n- Optional indexForAsyncInvocation() for spawning new indices\\n- Executed synchronously during cluster state update tasks\\n\\n**ClusterStateWaitStep** (ClusterStateWaitStep.java)\\n- Abstract class for steps that wait on cluster state conditions\\n- Method: isConditionMet(IndexMetadata, ClusterState) -> boolean\\n- Fast-path for checks not requiring async operations\\n- Included in periodic policy execution\\n\\n**AsyncWaitStep** (AsyncWaitStep.java)\\n- Abstract class for periodically-evaluated async conditions\\n- Method: evaluateCondition(Metadata, Index, Listener, TimeValue)\\n- Listener callbacks: onResponse(boolean, ToXContentObject) and onFailure(Exception)\\n- Used for checks that may require I/O (e.g., segment count, rollover readiness)\\n\\n**AsyncActionStep** (AsyncActionStep.java)\\n- Abstract class for async actions via transport/client calls\\n- Method: performAction(IndexMetadata, ClusterState, ClusterStateObserver, ActionListener<Void>)\\n- indexSurvives() indicates if action deletes the index\\n- Used for operations like rollover, shrink, force-merge, delete\\n\\n### 3. ILM Service (Policy Evaluator)\\n\\n**IndexLifecycleService** (x-pack/plugin/ilm/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/xpack/ilm/IndexLifecycleService.java)\\n- Implements ClusterStateListener, ClusterStateApplier, SchedulerEngine.Listener\\n- Triggers policy evaluation: scheduled via SchedulerEngine and on cluster changes\\n- Key method: clusterChanged(ClusterChangedEvent) - filters indices and dispatches to IndexLifecycleRunner\\n- Maintains master-only scheduler for policy evaluation\\n- Handles cluster state lifecycle events and index creation\\n- Integrates with PluginShutdownService for graceful shutdown\\n\\n### 4. Policy Execution Engine\\n\\n**IndexLifecycleRunner** (IndexLifecycleRunner.java)\\n- Executes individual step operations from PolicyStepsRegistry\\n- Dispatches step execution to appropriate method based on step type:\\n  - ClusterStateActionStep: submitUnbatchedTask(ExecuteStepsUpdateTask)\\n  - AsyncWaitStep: evaluateCondition() with periodic re-evaluation\\n  - AsyncActionStep: performAction() with client\\n  - ClusterStateWaitStep: immediate condition check\\n- Handles step failure, retry logic, and error step transitions\\n- Updates LifecycleExecutionState in index metadata\\n\\n**PolicyStepsRegistry** (PolicyStepsRegistry.java)\\n- Central registry mapping policy names to compiled step sequences\\n- Maintains: lifecycle policy map, first step cache, step lookups\\n- Updates dynamically as policies change via update(Metadata)\\n- Parses phase definitions from IndexLifecycleMetadata\\n- Provides: getStep(IndexMetadata, StepKey), getIndexAgeForPhase(policy, phase)\\n\\n### 5. Cluster State Update Tasks\\n\\n**ExecuteStepsUpdateTask** (ExecuteStepsUpdateTask.java)\\n- Extends IndexLifecycleClusterStateUpdateTask\\n- Executes ClusterStateActionStep operations\\n- Updates metadata with new step keys and execution state\\n- Deduplicates identical transitions\\n\\n**MoveToNextStepUpdateTask** (MoveToNextStepUpdateTask.java)\\n- Transitions index to next step after async action completes\\n- Updates LifecycleExecutionState with new StepKey\\n- Validates transition legality via IndexLifecycleTransition\\n\\n**IndexLifecycleTransition** (IndexLifecycleTransition.java)\\n- Validates step transitions and policy phase changes\\n- moveClusterStateToNextStep(IndexMetadata, StepKey) -> ClusterState\\n- Manages phase caching via PhaseCacheManagement\\n- Handles policy metadata versioning\\n\\n### 6. Execution State Storage\\n\\n**LifecycleExecutionState** (server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/metadata/LifecycleExecutionState.java)\\n- Record-based immutable state containing:\\n  - Current: phase, action, step\\n  - Error tracking: failedStep, isAutoRetryableError, failedStepRetryCount\\n  - Metadata: stepInfo, previousStepInfo, phaseDefinition\\n  - Timestamps: lifecycleDate, phaseTime, actionTime, stepTime\\n  - Context-specific: snapshotRepository, snapshotName, shrinkIndexName, downsampleIndexName\\n- Serialized as custom metadata in IndexMetadata via ILM_CUSTOM_METADATA_KEY (\\\"ilm\\\")\\n- Builder pattern for construction and modification\\n\\n**IndexMetadata** (server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/metadata/IndexMetadata.java)\\n- Contains LifecycleExecutionState via getLifecycleExecutionState()\\n- Stores lifecycle policy name and execution state\\n\\n### 7. Transport Actions (Policy Management)\\n\\n**TransportPutLifecycleAction** (x-pack/plugin/ilm/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/xpack/ilm/action/TransportPutLifecycleAction.java)\\n- Extends TransportMasterNodeAction<PutLifecycleRequest, AcknowledgedResponse>\\n- Handler: UpdateLifecyclePolicyTask (AckedClusterStateUpdateTask)\\n- Validates policy format via NamedXContentRegistry\\n- Updates IndexLifecycleMetadata in cluster state\\n- Handles policy version management and index metadata updates\\n- Supports SearchableSnapshotAction and WaitForSnapshotAction validation\\n\\n**TransportDeleteLifecycleAction** (x-pack/plugin/ilm/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/xpack/ilm/action/TransportDeleteLifecycleAction.java)\\n- Extends TransportMasterNodeAction<Request, AcknowledgedResponse>\\n- Handler: DeleteLifecyclePolicyTask (AckedClusterStateUpdateTask)\\n- Removes policy from IndexLifecycleMetadata\\n- Ensures no indices reference deleted policy\\n\\n### 8. Additional ILM Actions\\n\\n- TransportGetLifecycleAction: Retrieves stored policies\\n- TransportExplainLifecycleAction: Explains current execution state of indices\\n- TransportMoveToStepAction: Manual step transitions\\n- TransportRetryAction: Retries failed steps\\n\\n### 9. Metadata Management\\n\\n**IndexLifecycleMetadata** (IndexLifecycleMetadata.java)\\n- Cluster-wide container for all ILM policies\\n- Map<String, LifecyclePolicyMetadata> storing policy metadata\\n- OperationMode tracking (RUNNING, STOPPED, STOPPING)\\n\\n**LifecyclePolicyMetadata** (LifecyclePolicyMetadata.java)\\n- Wrapper around LifecyclePolicy with versioning\\n- Stores: policy name, version, creation time, last modified time\\n- Used for policy update detection and consistency\\n\\n**PhaseCacheManagement** (PhaseCacheManagement.java)\\n- Manages cached phase definitions in index metadata\\n- Prevents execution interruption when policies are updated\\n- Methods: refreshPhaseDefinition(), updateIndicesForPolicy()\\n\\n## Execution Flow\\n\\n1. **Policy Trigger**: IndexLifecycleService.clusterChanged() fires on cluster events or scheduled interval\\n2. **Index Discovery**: Service filters indices with active ILM policies\\n3. **Registry Lookup**: PolicyStepsRegistry provides current step for index\\n4. **Step Execution**:\\n   - ClusterStateActionStep: Immediate cluster state update via ExecuteStepsUpdateTask\\n   - AsyncActionStep: Client action dispatch via IndexLifecycleRunner\\n   - AsyncWaitStep: Async condition evaluation with periodic re-check\\n   - ClusterStateWaitStep: Immediate condition evaluation\\n5. **State Transition**: Successful step completion triggers MoveToNextStepUpdateTask\\n6. **Error Handling**: Failures transition to ErrorStep and await manual retry\\n\\n## Key Design Patterns\\n\\n- **Immutable State**: LifecycleExecutionState is a record for thread-safe mutations\\n- **Registry Pattern**: PolicyStepsRegistry centralizes step lookup and caching\\n- **Composite Steps**: Steps compose into phases and policies\\n- **Listener Pattern**: AsyncWaitStep and AsyncActionStep use callbacks for completion\\n- **Cluster Task Deduplication**: IndexLifecycleRunner deduplicates identical transitions\\n- **Phase Caching**: PhaseCacheManagement isolates indices from concurrent policy updates\\n\"\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":12,"cache_creation_input_tokens":8581,"cache_read_input_tokens":92753,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":8581,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8564d298-49ab-4123-bada-c2da8fbed270","uuid":"74e384ad-7467-437e-ba77-7fbb43992d7b"}

\"server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/metadata/LifecycleExecutionState.java\",\n      \"symbol\": \"LifecycleExecutionState\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"text\": \"# Elasticsearch Index Lifecycle Management (ILM) Architecture Analysis\\n\\n## Overview\\nThe Elasticsearch 8.17.0 ILM system is a comprehensive policy-driven index management framework. The implementation spans across three main components: core policy definitions, ILM coordination service, and cluster state management.\\n\\n## Core Components\\n\\n### 1. Policy Model Classes (x-pack/plugin/core/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/xpack/core/ilm/)\\n\\n**LifecyclePolicy** (LifecyclePolicy.java)\\n- Represents the lifecycle of an index from creation to deletion\\n- Contains a mapping of phase names to Phase objects\\n- Implements SimpleDiffable<LifecyclePolicy> and ToXContentObject for serialization\\n- Supports TimeseriesLifecycleType and validates phases through LifecycleType\\n- Includes optional metadata and deprecation flags\\n\\n**Phase** (Phase.java)\\n- Represents a set of LifecycleActions to execute at a particular point in index lifecycle\\n- Contains minimum age (TimeValue) and a map of named LifecycleActions\\n- Implements ToXContentObject and Writeable for serialization\\n- Ordered by LifecycleType; actions execute in defined order\\n\\n**Step** (Step.java)\\n- Abstract base class for all ILM execution steps\\n- Contains StepKey (phase, action, name) and next step reference\\n- Subclassed into four main step types:\\n  - ClusterStateActionStep: Updates cluster state, immediate execution\\n  - ClusterStateWaitStep: Waits for cluster state condition\\n  - AsyncActionStep: Performs asynchronous actions via client\\n  - AsyncWaitStep: Periodically checks asynchronous conditions\\n\\n### 2. Step Execution Framework\\n\\n**ClusterStateActionStep** (ClusterStateActionStep.java)\\n- Abstract class for steps that modify cluster state directly\\n- Method: performAction(Index, ClusterState) -> ClusterState\\n- Optional indexForAsyncInvocation() for spawning new indices\\n- Executed synchronously during cluster state update tasks\\n\\n**ClusterStateWaitStep** (ClusterStateWaitStep.java)\\n- Abstract class for steps that wait on cluster state conditions\\n- Method: isConditionMet(IndexMetadata, ClusterState) -> boolean\\n- Fast-path for checks not requiring async operations\\n- Included in periodic policy execution\\n\\n**AsyncWaitStep** (AsyncWaitStep.java)\\n- Abstract class for periodically-evaluated async conditions\\n- Method: evaluateCondition(Metadata, Index, Listener, TimeValue)\\n- Listener callbacks: onResponse(boolean, ToXContentObject) and onFailure(Exception)\\n- Used for checks that may require I/O (e.g., segment count, rollover readiness)\\n\\n**AsyncActionStep** (AsyncActionStep.java)\\n- Abstract class for async actions via transport/client calls\\n- Method: performAction(IndexMetadata, ClusterState, ClusterStateObserver, ActionListener<Void>)\\n- indexSurvives() indicates if action deletes the index\\n- Used for operations like rollover, shrink, force-merge, delete\\n\\n### 3. ILM Service (Policy Evaluator)\\n\\n**IndexLifecycleService** (x-pack/plugin/ilm/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/xpack/ilm/IndexLifecycleService.java)\\n- Implements ClusterStateListener, ClusterStateApplier, SchedulerEngine.Listener\\n- Triggers policy evaluation: scheduled via SchedulerEngine and on cluster changes\\n- Key method: clusterChanged(ClusterChangedEvent) - filters indices and dispatches to IndexLifecycleRunner\\n- Maintains master-only scheduler for policy evaluation\\n- Handles cluster state lifecycle events and index creation\\n- Integrates with PluginShutdownService for graceful shutdown\\n\\n### 4. Policy Execution Engine\\n\\n**IndexLifecycleRunner** (IndexLifecycleRunner.java)\\n- Executes individual step operations from PolicyStepsRegistry\\n- Dispatches step execution to appropriate method based on step type:\\n  - ClusterStateActionStep: submitUnbatchedTask(ExecuteStepsUpdateTask)\\n  - AsyncWaitStep: evaluateCondition() with periodic re-evaluation\\n  - AsyncActionStep: performAction() with client\\n  - ClusterStateWaitStep: immediate condition check\\n- Handles step failure, retry logic, and error step transitions\\n- Updates LifecycleExecutionState in index metadata\\n\\n**PolicyStepsRegistry** (PolicyStepsRegistry.java)\\n- Central registry mapping policy names to compiled step sequences\\n- Maintains: lifecycle policy map, first step cache, step lookups\\n- Updates dynamically as policies change via update(Metadata)\\n- Parses phase definitions from IndexLifecycleMetadata\\n- Provides: getStep(IndexMetadata, StepKey), getIndexAgeForPhase(policy, phase)\\n\\n### 5. Cluster State Update Tasks\\n\\n**ExecuteStepsUpdateTask** (ExecuteStepsUpdateTask.java)\\n- Extends IndexLifecycleClusterStateUpdateTask\\n- Executes ClusterStateActionStep operations\\n- Updates metadata with new step keys and execution state\\n- Deduplicates identical transitions\\n\\n**MoveToNextStepUpdateTask** (MoveToNextStepUpdateTask.java)\\n- Transitions index to next step after async action completes\\n- Updates LifecycleExecutionState with new StepKey\\n- Validates transition legality via IndexLifecycleTransition\\n\\n**IndexLifecycleTransition** (IndexLifecycleTransition.java)\\n- Validates step transitions and policy phase changes\\n- moveClusterStateToNextStep(IndexMetadata, StepKey) -> ClusterState\\n- Manages phase caching via PhaseCacheManagement\\n- Handles policy metadata versioning\\n\\n### 6. Execution State Storage\\n\\n**LifecycleExecutionState** (server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/metadata/LifecycleExecutionState.java)\\n- Record-based immutable state containing:\\n  - Current: phase, action, step\\n  - Error tracking: failedStep, isAutoRetryableError, failedStepRetryCount\\n  - Metadata: stepInfo, previousStepInfo, phaseDefinition\\n  - Timestamps: lifecycleDate, phaseTime, actionTime, stepTime\\n  - Context-specific: snapshotRepository, snapshotName, shrinkIndexName, downsampleIndexName\\n- Serialized as custom metadata in IndexMetadata via ILM_CUSTOM_METADATA_KEY (\\\"ilm\\\")\\n- Builder pattern for construction and modification\\n\\n**IndexMetadata** (server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/metadata/IndexMetadata.java)\\n- Contains LifecycleExecutionState via getLifecycleExecutionState()\\n- Stores lifecycle policy name and execution state\\n\\n### 7. Transport Actions (Policy Management)\\n\\n**TransportPutLifecycleAction** (x-pack/plugin/ilm/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/xpack/ilm/action/TransportPutLifecycleAction.java)\\n- Extends TransportMasterNodeAction<PutLifecycleRequest, AcknowledgedResponse>\\n- Handler: UpdateLifecyclePolicyTask (AckedClusterStateUpdateTask)\\n- Validates policy format via NamedXContentRegistry\\n- Updates IndexLifecycleMetadata in cluster state\\n- Handles policy version management and index metadata updates\\n- Supports SearchableSnapshotAction and WaitForSnapshotAction validation\\n\\n**TransportDeleteLifecycleAction** (x-pack/plugin/ilm/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/xpack/ilm/action/TransportDeleteLifecycleAction.java)\\n- Extends TransportMasterNodeAction<Request, AcknowledgedResponse>\\n- Handler: DeleteLifecyclePolicyTask (AckedClusterStateUpdateTask)\\n- Removes policy from IndexLifecycleMetadata\\n- Ensures no indices reference deleted policy\\n\\n### 8. Additional ILM Actions\\n\\n- TransportGetLifecycleAction: Retrieves stored policies\\n- TransportExplainLifecycleAction: Explains current execution state of indices\\n- TransportMoveToStepAction: Manual step transitions\\n- TransportRetryAction: Retries failed steps\\n\\n### 9. Metadata Management\\n\\n**IndexLifecycleMetadata** (IndexLifecycleMetadata.java)\\n- Cluster-wide container for all ILM policies\\n- Map<String, LifecyclePolicyMetadata> storing policy metadata\\n- OperationMode tracking (RUNNING, STOPPED, STOPPING)\\n\\n**LifecyclePolicyMetadata** (LifecyclePolicyMetadata.java)\\n- Wrapper around LifecyclePolicy with versioning\\n- Stores: policy name, version, creation time, last modified time\\n- Used for policy update detection and consistency\\n\\n**PhaseCacheManagement** (PhaseCacheManagement.java)\\n- Manages cached phase definitions in index metadata\\n- Prevents execution interruption when policies are updated\\n- Methods: refreshPhaseDefinition(), updateIndicesForPolicy()\\n\\n## Execution Flow\\n\\n1. **Policy Trigger**: IndexLifecycleService.clusterChanged() fires on cluster events or scheduled interval\\n2. **Index Discovery**: Service filters indices with active ILM policies\\n3. **Registry Lookup**: PolicyStepsRegistry provides current step for index\\n4. **Step Execution**:\\n   - ClusterStateActionStep: Immediate cluster state update via ExecuteStepsUpdateTask\\n   - AsyncActionStep: Client action dispatch via IndexLifecycleRunner\\n   - AsyncWaitStep: Async condition evaluation with periodic re-check\\n   - ClusterStateWaitStep: Immediate condition evaluation\\n5. **State Transition**: Successful step completion triggers MoveToNextStepUpdateTask\\n6. **Error Handling**: Failures transition to ErrorStep and await manual retry\\n\\n## Key Design Patterns\\n\\n- **Immutable State**: LifecycleExecutionState is a record for thread-safe mutations\\n- **Registry Pattern**: PolicyStepsRegistry centralizes step lookup and caching\\n- **Composite Steps**: Steps compose into phases and policies\\n- **Listener Pattern**: AsyncWaitStep and AsyncActionStep use callbacks for completion\\n- **Cluster Task Deduplication**: IndexLifecycleRunner deduplicates identical transitions\\n- **Phase Caching**: PhaseCacheManagement isolates indices from concurrent policy updates\\n\"\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
